I can’t really think of another time that was like this for weeks.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any unexplained weight gain or increased sensitivity to cold recently?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t noticed any weight gain, and I don’t think I’ve been more sensitive to cold than usual.\n\nDoctor: After you physically or mentally exert yourself—even something mild like folding laundry—do you notice that your exhaustion gets significantly worse for the next day or two?\n\nPatient: Yes.
